   world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", n_iters);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Build the unstructured mesh\n    printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n    \n    // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    runSimulation(world, n_iters);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    \n    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;\n    \n    printf(\"Performance:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\\n\", time_per_iter);\n    printf(\"  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\\n\", giga_elems_per_sec);\n    printf(\"  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Compute hash for verification\n    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);\n    printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);\n        }\n        print_results(energyData, \"ElementEnergy\");\n    }\n    \n    //
